##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/db.py` (ACTUALIZADO - agrega tablas admin)

Agrega estas clases al archivo `db.py` existente:

```python
from datetime import datetime
from passlib.context import CryptContext

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")

class Admin(Base):
    __tablename__ = "admins"
    
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(unique=True, index=True)
    hashed_password: Mapped[str]
    full_name: Mapped[str]
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(default=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(default=datetime.utcnow)

class CatalogItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "catalog"
    
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str]
    description: Mapped[str]
    price: Mapped[float]
    currency: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(default="COP")
    category: Mapped[str]  # "plataforma" | "combo" | "addon"
    platform: Mapped[str | None]  # netflix, disney, etc.
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(default=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

class ChatIntervention(Base):
    """Registra cuando un humano interviene en un chat."""
    __tablename__ = "chat_interventions"
    
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    phone: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(index=True)
    admin_id: Mapped[int]
    started_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(default=datetime.utcnow)
    ended_at: Mapped[datetime | None]
    reason: Mapped[str | None]

async def seed_admin():
    """Crea un admin por defecto si no existe."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        existing = await session.execute(
            select(Admin).where(Admin.username == "admin")
        )
        if not existing.scalar_one_or_none():
            admin = Admin(
                username="admin",
                hashed_password=pwd_context.hash("admin123"),  # CAMBIAR EN PRODUCCIÓN
                full_name="Administrador",
                is_active=True
            )
            session.add(admin)
            await session.commit()
            print("👤 [DB] Admin por defecto creado: admin / admin123")

async def seed_catalog():
    """Carga el catálogo inicial desde el RAG."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        existing = await session.execute(select(CatalogItem))
        if existing.scalar_one_or_none():
            return  # Ya existe
        
        items = [
            CatalogItem(id="netflix_premium", name="Netflix Premium", description="4 pantallas, 4K UHD", price=15000, category="plataforma", platform="netflix"),
            CatalogItem(id="netflix_standard", name="Netflix Estándar", description="2 pantallas, Full HD", price=10000, category="plataforma", platform="netflix"),
            CatalogItem(id="disney_premium", name="Disney+ Premium", description="4 pantallas, 4K, incluye ESPN+", price=12000, category="plataforma", platform="disney"),
            CatalogItem(id="hbo_standard", name="Max (HBO)", description="2 pantallas, Full HD", price=10000, category="plataforma", platform="hbo"),
            CatalogItem(id="spotify_individual", name="Spotify Premium", description="Individual, sin anuncios", price=8000, category="plataforma", platform="spotify"),
            CatalogItem(id="youtube_premium", name="YouTube Premium", description="Sin anuncios + YouTube Music", price=7000, category="plataforma", platform="youtube"),
            CatalogItem(id="combo_familiar", name="Combo Familiar", description="Netflix + Disney+ + Max", price=32000, category="combo", platform="combo"),
        ]
        
        session.add_all(items)
        await session.commit()
        print(f"📦 [DB] Catálogo cargado: {len(items)} items")
```

Actualiza la función `init_db()` para llamar a estos seeds:

```python
async def init_db():
    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
    await seed_admin()
    await seed_catalog()
    print("✅ [DB] Base de datos inicializada.")
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/auth.py` (NUEVO)

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPBearer, HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
from sqlalchemy import select
from app.config import settings
from app.db import async_session, Admin

SECRET_KEY = settings.jwt_secret_key
ALGORITHM = "HS256"
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 60 * 24  # 24 horas

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
security = HTTPBearer()

def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)

def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None) -> str:
    to_encode = data.copy()
    expire = datetime.utcnow() + (expires_delta or timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES))
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    return jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)

async def authenticate_user(username: str, password: str) -> Admin | None:
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(select(Admin).where(Admin.username == username))
        admin = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if not admin or not verify_password(password, admin.hashed_password):
            return None
        if not admin.is_active:
            return None
        
        return admin

async def get_current_admin(credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security)) -> Admin:
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Token inválido o expirado",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )
    
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(credentials.credentials, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        username: str = payload.get("sub")
        if username is None:
            raise credentials_exception
    except JWTError:
        raise credentials_exception
    
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(select(Admin).where(Admin.username == username))
        admin = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if admin is None or not admin.is_active:
            raise credentials_exception
        
        return admin
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/models.py` (NUEVO)

```python
from pydantic import BaseModel
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str

class TokenResponse(BaseModel):
    access_token: str
    token_type: str = "bearer"
    admin: dict

class CatalogItemCreate(BaseModel):
    id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    price: float
    currency: str = "COP"
    category: str
    platform: Optional[str] = None

class CatalogItemUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    description: Optional[str] = None
    price: Optional[float] = None
    is_active: Optional[bool] = None

class ClientUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    subscription_status: Optional[str] = None

class DashboardStats(BaseModel):
    total_clients: int
    active_conversations: int
    today_messages: int
    today_sales: int
    bot_resolution_rate: float  # % de chats resueltos sin humano

class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    id: int
    phone: str
    role: str
    content: str
    created_at: datetime
    is_intervention: bool = False

class ConversationSummary(BaseModel):
    phone: str
    client_name: str
    last_message: str
    last_message_time: datetime
    message_count: int
    is_being_intervened: bool
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/services.py` (NUEVO)

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from sqlalchemy import select, func, and_
from app.db import async_session, Client, Conversation, CatalogItem, ChatIntervention

async def get_dashboard_stats() -> dict:
    today = datetime.utcnow().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Total clientes
        total_clients = await session.execute(select(func.count(Client.id)))
        total_clients = total_clients.scalar() or 0
        
        # Conversaciones activas (últimas 24h)
        active_convos = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(func.distinct(Conversation.phone)))
            .where(Conversation.created_at >= today)
        )
        active_convos = active_convos.scalar() or 0
        
        # Mensajes de hoy
        today_msgs = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(Conversation.id))
            .where(Conversation.created_at >= today)
        )
        today_msgs = today_msgs.scalar() or 0
        
        # Ventas de hoy (buscar en mensajes con intención "sales" completada)
        # Simplificado: contar mensajes del bot con palabras clave de venta
        today_sales = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(Conversation.id))
            .where(
                and_(
                    Conversation.created_at >= today,
                    Conversation.role == "assistant",
                    Conversation.content.ilike("%confirmar tu compra%")
                )
            )
        )
        today_sales = today_sales.scalar() or 0
        
        # Tasa de resolución del bot
        total_interventions = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(ChatIntervention.id))
            .where(ChatIntervention.started_at >= today)
        )
        total_interventions = total_interventions.scalar() or 0
        
        resolution_rate = ((active_convos - total_interventions) / active_convos * 100) if active_convos > 0 else 100
        
        return {
            "total_clients": total_clients,
            "active_conversations": active_convos,
            "today_messages": today_msgs,
            "today_sales": today_sales,
            "bot_resolution_rate": round(resolution_rate, 1)
        }

async def get_conversations_list() -> list[dict]:
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Obtener teléfonos únicos con su último mensaje
        result = await session.execute(
            select(
                Conversation.phone,
                func.max(Conversation.created_at).label("last_time")
            )
            .group_by(Conversation.phone)
            .order_by(func.max(Conversation.created_at).desc())
            .limit(50)
        )
        
        conversations = []
        for row in result:
            phone = row.phone
            last_time = row.last_time
            
            # Buscar cliente
            client_result = await session.execute(
                select(Client).where(Client.phone == phone)
            )
            client = client_result.scalar_one_or_none()
            client_name = client.name if client else "Desconocido"
            
            # Último mensaje
            last_msg_result = await session.execute(
                select(Conversation)
                .where(Conversation.phone == phone)
                .order_by(Conversation.created_at.desc())
                .limit(1)
            )
            last_msg = last_msg_result.scalar_one_or_none()
            
            # Contar mensajes
            count_result = await session.execute(
                select(func.count(Conversation.id))
                .where(Conversation.phone == phone)
            )
            msg_count = count_result.scalar() or 0
            
            # Verificar si hay intervención activa
            intervention_result = await session.execute(
                select(ChatIntervention)
                .where(
                    and_(
                        ChatIntervention.phone == phone,
                        ChatIntervention.ended_at == None
                    )
                )
            )
            is_intervened = intervention_result.scalar_one_or_none() is not None
            
            conversations.append({
                "phone": phone,
                "client_name": client_name,
                "last_message": last_msg.content if last_msg else "",
                "last_message_time": last_time,
                "message_count": msg_count,
                "is_being_intervened": is_intervened
            })
        
        return conversations

async def get_conversation_history(phone: str) -> list[dict]:
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            select(Conversation)
            .where(Conversation.phone == phone)
            .order_by(Conversation.created_at.asc())
        )
        messages = result.scalars().all()
        
        return [
            {
                "id": m.id,
                "phone": m.phone,
                "role": m.role,
                "content": m.content,
                "created_at": m.created_at,
                "is_intervention": False
            }
            for m in messages
        ]

async def add_admin_message(phone: str, content: str, admin_id: int):
    """Agrega un mensaje de un admin (intervención humana)."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Crear intervención si no existe
        intervention_result = await session.execute(
            select(ChatIntervention)
            .where(
                and_(
                    ChatIntervention.phone == phone,
                    ChatIntervention.ended_at == None
                )
            )
        )
        intervention = intervention_result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if not intervention:
            intervention = ChatIntervention(phone=phone, admin_id=admin_id)
            session.add(intervention)
        
        # Guardar mensaje
        msg = Conversation(phone=phone, role="assistant", content=f"[HUMANO] {content}")
        session.add(msg)
        await session.commit()

async def end_intervention(phone: str):
    """Finaliza la intervención humana en un chat."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            select(ChatIntervention)
            .where(
                and_(
                    ChatIntervention.phone == phone,
                    ChatIntervention.ended_at == None
                )
            )
        )
        intervention = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if intervention:
            intervention.ended_at = datetime.utcnow()
            await session.commit()

async def get_all_catalog() -> list[dict]:
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(select(CatalogItem))
        items = result.scalars().all()
        return [
            {
                "id": item.id,
                "name": item.name,
                "description": item.description,
                "price": item.price,
                "currency": item.currency,
                "category": item.category,
                "platform": item.platform,
                "is_active": item.is_active
            }
            for item in items
        ]

async def update_catalog_item(item_id: str, updates: dict):
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            select(CatalogItem).where(CatalogItem.id == item_id)
        )
        item = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if not item:
            return None
        
        for key, value in updates.items():
            if value is not None:
                setattr(item, key, value)
        
        await session.commit()
        return item

async def get_all_clients() -> list[dict]:
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(select(Client))
        clients = result.scalars().all()
        return [
            {
                "id": c.id,
                "phone": c.phone,
                "name": c.name,
                "subscription_status": c.subscription_status,
                "last_purchase": c.last_purchase
            }
            for c in clients
        ]

async def update_client(client_id: int, updates: dict):
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            select(Client).where(Client.id == client_id)
        )
        client = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if not client:
            return None
        
        for key, value in updates.items():
            if value is not None:
                setattr(client, key, value)
        
        await session.commit()
        return client
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/router.py` (NUEVO)

```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from app.admin.auth import authenticate_user, create_access_token, get_current_admin
from app.admin.models import (
    LoginRequest, TokenResponse, CatalogItemCreate, 
    CatalogItemUpdate, ClientUpdate, DashboardStats
)
from app.admin.services import (
    get_dashboard_stats, get_conversations_list, get_conversation_history,
    add_admin_message, end_intervention, get_all_catalog, update_catalog_item,
    get_all_clients, update_client
)
from app.db import Admin, CatalogItem, Client, async_session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/admin", tags=["admin"])

@router.post("/auth/login", response_model=TokenResponse)
async def login(request: LoginRequest):
    admin = await authenticate_user(request.username, request.password)
    if not admin:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Credenciales inválidas")
    
    access_token = create_access_token(data={"sub": admin.username})
    
    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "admin": {
            "username": admin.username,
            "full_name": admin.full_name
        }
    }

@router.get("/auth/me")
async def get_me(admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    return {
        "username": admin.username,
        "full_name": admin.full_name
    }

@router.get("/dashboard/stats", response_model=DashboardStats)
async def dashboard_stats(admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    return await get_dashboard_stats()

@router.get("/conversations")
async def list_conversations(admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    return await get_conversations_list()

@router.get("/conversations/{phone}/history")
async def conversation_history(phone: str, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    return await get_conversation_history(phone)

@router.post("/conversations/{phone}/message")
async def send_admin_message(
    phone: str, 
    content: str,
    admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    await add_admin_message(phone, content, admin.id)
    
    # Enviar al gateway vía Redis
    import redis.asyncio as redis
    from app.config import settings
    redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
    
    import json
    await redis_client.lpush("queue:outgoing_messages", json.dumps({
        "message_id": f"admin_{admin.id}_{phone}",
        "phone": phone,
        "content": content,
        "action": "send_message",
        "delay_ms": 0
    }))
    
    return {"status": "sent"}

@router.post("/conversations/{phone}/end-intervention")
async def end_chat_intervention(phone: str, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    await end_intervention(phone)
    return {"status": "ended"}

@router.get("/catalog")
async def list_catalog(admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    return await get_all_catalog()

@router.put("/catalog/{item_id}")
async def update_item(
    item_id: str, 
    updates: CatalogItemUpdate,
    admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    item = await update_catalog_item(item_id, updates.dict(exclude_unset=True))
    if not item:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Item no encontrado")
    
    # TODO: Sincronizar con ChromaDB (RAG)
    
    return {"status": "updated", "item": item}

@router.get("/clients")
async def list_clients(admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    return await get_all_clients()

@router.put("/clients/{client_id}")
async def update_client_info(
    client_id: int,
    updates: ClientUpdate,
    admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    client = await update_client(client_id, updates.dict(exclude_unset=True))
    if not client:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Cliente no encontrado")
    return {"status": "updated", "client": client}
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/websocket.py` (NUEVO)

```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect, Depends
from typing import List
import redis.asyncio as redis
import asyncio
import json
from app.config import settings
from app.admin.auth import get_current_admin
from app.db import Admin

router = APIRouter()

class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: List[WebSocket] = []
    
    async def connect(self, websocket: WebSocket):
        await websocket.accept()
        self.active_connections.append(websocket)
    
    def disconnect(self, websocket: WebSocket):
        self.active_connections.remove(websocket)
    
    async def broadcast(self, message: dict):
        for connection in self.active_connections:
            try:
                await connection.send_json(message)
            except:
                pass

manager = ConnectionManager()

@router.websocket("/ws/chats")
async def websocket_chats(websocket: WebSocket):
    await manager.connect(websocket)
    
    # Suscribirse a Redis Pub/Sub para recibir eventos en tiempo real
    redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
    pubsub = redis_client.pubsub()
    await pubsub.subscribe("channel:chat_events")
    
    try:
        # Tarea para escuchar Redis
        async def listen_redis():
            try:
                async for message in pubsub.listen():
                    if message["type"] == "message":
                        data = json.loads(message["data"])
                        await manager.broadcast(data)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ [WebSocket] Error escuchando Redis: {e}")
        
        redis_task = asyncio.create_task(listen_redis())
        
        # Mantener conexión viva
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            # El cliente puede enviar pings para mantener la conexión
            if data == "ping":
                await websocket.send_json({"type": "pong"})
    
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(websocket)
        redis_task.cancel()
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/main.py` (ACTUALIZADO)

```python
import asyncio
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from pathlib import Path
from app.worker import start_worker
from app.db import init_db
from app.admin.router import router as admin_router
from app.admin.websocket import router as ws_router

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    await init_db()
    worker_task = asyncio.create_task(start_worker())
    print("✅ [Orchestrator] Worker + Admin API iniciados.")
    yield
    worker_task.cancel()

app = FastAPI(title="Streaming Bot Orchestrator", lifespan=lifespan)

# Incluir routers
app.include_router(admin_router)
app.include_router(ws_router)

# Servir frontend estático
static_path = Path(__file__).parent / "static"
if static_path.exists():
    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=static_path / "assets"), name="static")
    
    @app.get("/{full_path:path}")
    async def serve_frontend(full_path: str):
        file_path = static_path / full_path
        if file_path.exists() and file_path.is_file():
            return FileResponse(file_path)
        return FileResponse(static_path / "index.html")

@app.get("/health")
async def health():
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "orchestrator"}
```

```jsx
import { Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useAuth } from './hooks/useAuth'
import Login from './pages/Login'
import Dashboard from './pages/Dashboard'
import Conversations from './pages/Conversations'
import Catalog from './pages/Catalog'
import Clients from './pages/Clients'
import Sidebar from './components/layout/Sidebar'
import Header from './components/layout/Header'

function ProtectedRoute({ children }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth()
  
  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-screen">Cargando...</div>
  }
  
  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-gray-50">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
      <Route path="/" element={<ProtectedRoute><Dashboard /></ProtectedRoute>} />
      <Route path="/conversations" element={<ProtectedRoute><Conversations /></ProtectedRoute>} />
      <Route path="/catalog" element={<ProtectedRoute><Catalog /></ProtectedRoute>} />
      <Route path="/clients" element={<ProtectedRoute><Clients /></ProtectedRoute>} />
    </Routes>
  )
}
```

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/context/AuthContext.jsx`

```jsx
import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import api from '../api/client'

const AuthContext = createContext(null)

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
    if (token) {
      api.get('/api/admin/auth/me')
        .then(res => setUser(res.data))
        .catch(() => localStorage.removeItem('token'))
        .finally(() => setLoading(false))
    } else {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])
  
  const login = async (username, password) => {
    const res = await api.post('/api/admin/auth/login', { username, password })
    localStorage.setItem('token', res.data.access_token)
    setUser(res.data.admin)
    return res.data
  }
  
  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('token')
    setUser(null)
  }
  
  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, login, logout, loading }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext)
```

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/api/client.js`

```javascript
import axios from 'axios'

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: '',
})

api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  }
  return config
})

api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('token')
      window.location.href = '/login'
    }
    return Promise.reject(error)
  }
)

export default api
```

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/api/websocket.js`

```javascript
class ChatWebSocket {
  constructor() {
    this.ws = null
    this.listeners = []
    this.reconnectAttempts = 0
    this.maxReconnectAttempts = 5
  }
  
  connect() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
    const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:'
    const wsUrl = `${protocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/chats?token=${token}`
    
    this.ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl)
    
    this.ws.onopen = () => {
      console.log('✅ WebSocket conectado')
      this.reconnectAttempts = 0
      
      // Ping cada 30 segundos para mantener conexión
      this.pingInterval = setInterval(() => {
        if (this.ws?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
          this.ws.send('ping')
        }
      }, 30000)
    }
    
    this.ws.onmessage = (event) => {
      const data = JSON.parse(event.data)
      this.listeners.forEach(listener => listener(data))
    }
    
    this.ws.onclose = () => {
      console.log('❌ WebSocket desconectado')
      clearInterval(this.pingInterval)
      
      if (this.reconnectAttempts < this.maxReconnectAttempts) {
        this.reconnectAttempts++
        console.log(`🔄 Reintentando conexión (${this.reconnectAttempts}/${this.maxReconnectAttempts})...`)
        setTimeout(() => this.connect(), 3000)
      }
    }
    
    this.ws.onerror = (error) => {
      console.error('WebSocket error:', error)
    }
  }
  
  disconnect() {
    if (this.ws) {
      this.ws.close()
      clearInterval(this.pingInterval)
    }
  }
  
  subscribe(listener) {
    this.listeners.push(listener)
    return () => {
      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter(l => l !== listener)
    }
  }
}

export const chatWs = new ChatWebSocket()
```

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/pages/Login.jsx`

```jsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth'
import { MessageSquare, Lock, User } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Login() {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const { login } = useAuth()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  
  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    setLoading(true)
    
    try {
      await login(username, password)
      navigate('/')
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Credenciales inválidas')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-indigo-100">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-lg shadow-xl w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <div className="bg-primary-500 p-3 rounded-full">
            <MessageSquare className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center mb-2">Panel de Administración</h1>
        <p className="text-gray-500 text-center mb-6">Streaming Bot Manager</p>
        
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Usuario</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <User className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-transparent"
                placeholder="admin"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Contraseña</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-transparent"
                placeholder="••••••••"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          {error && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 text-red-600 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}
          
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full bg-primary-500 hover:bg-primary-600 text-white font-medium py-2 rounded-lg transition disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? 'Ingresando...' : 'Iniciar Sesión'}
          </button>
        </form>
        
        <div className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
          <p>Credenciales por defecto:</p>
          <p className="font-mono">admin / admin123</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/pages/Conversations.jsx`

```jsx
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import api from '../api/client'
import { chatWs } from '../api/websocket'
import ChatList from '../components/chats/ChatList'
import ChatWindow from '../components/chats/ChatWindow'

export default function Conversations() {
  const [conversations, setConversations] = useState([])
  const [selectedPhone, setSelectedPhone] = useState(null)
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([])
  
  useEffect(() => {
    loadConversations()
    
    // Suscribirse a eventos en tiempo real
    const unsubscribe = chatWs.subscribe((data) => {
      if (data.type === 'new_message') {
        loadConversations()
        if (selectedPhone === data.phone) {
          loadMessages(selectedPhone)
        }
      }
    })
    
    chatWs.connect()
    
    return () => {
      unsubscribe()
    }
  }, [selectedPhone])
  
  const loadConversations = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/api/admin/conversations')
      setConversations(res.data)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }
  
  const loadMessages = async (phone) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/api/admin/conversations/${phone}/history`)
      setMessages(res.data)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }
  
  const handleSelectConversation = (phone) => {
    setSelectedPhone(phone)
    loadMessages(phone)
  }
  
  const handleSendMessage = async (content) => {
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/admin/conversations/${selectedPhone}/message`, null, {
        params: { content }
      })
      loadMessages(selectedPhone)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }
  
  const handleEndIntervention = async () => {
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/admin/conversations/${selectedPhone}/end-intervention`)
      loadConversations()
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }
  
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6">Conversaciones en Vivo</h1>
      
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow overflow-hidden flex h-[calc(100vh-200px)]">
        <div className="w-96 border-r overflow-y-auto">
          <ChatList 
            conversations={conversations} 
            selectedPhone={selectedPhone}
            onSelect={handleSelectConversation}
          />
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col">
          {selectedPhone ? (
            <ChatWindow 
              phone={selectedPhone}
              messages={messages}
              onSendMessage={handleSendMessage}
              onEndIntervention={handleEndIntervention}
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400">
              Selecciona una conversación
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

### USER
avanzamos al Sprint 4 (Multi-número con rotación automática

### ASSISTANT
Luz verde del CTO para el **Sprint 4: Multi-número con rotación automática**. Este sprint es el que convierte nuestro sistema de un "experimento frágil" a una **infraestructura de producción resiliente**.

---

### 🏢 1. Directiva del CTO

> "Este sprint es **crítico para la continuidad del negocio**. En el nicho de streaming, los números caen. No es una cuestión de 'si', sino de 'cuándo'. Las prioridades son:
> 1. **Cero downtime perceptible:** Si un número cae, el tráfico se redirige en segundos sin que el cliente lo note.
> 2. **Sticky sessions obligatorias:** Un cliente siempre habla con el mismo número. Esto es clave para no activar filtros anti-spam de Meta.
> 3. **Health scoring:** No todos los números son iguales. Un número nuevo no puede recibir 500 mensajes el primer día.
> 4. **Detección temprana de baneos:** Si un número cae con `loggedOut`, lo marcamos como 'quemado' y lo sacamos del pool automáticamente.
> 5. **Panel de control:** Debo ver en tiempo real qué números están activos, cuántos chats maneja cada uno, y poder 'quemar' uno manualmente si lo sospecho."

---

### 📐 2. Arquitectura del Sistema Multi-Número (Arquitecto)

#### 2.1. Diagrama de Flujo Actualizado

```
Cliente A (57300111) ──┐
Cliente B (57300222) ──┤
Cliente C (57300333) ──┼──► ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
Cliente D (57300444) ──┤    │   GATEWAY (Multi-Connection)     │
Cliente E (57300555) ──┘    │                                  │
                            │  ┌────────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌──────┐ │
                            │  │ WA #1  │ │ WA #2  │ │ WA #N│ │
                            │  │(Activo)│ │(Activo)│ │(QR)  │ │
                            │  │score:95│ │score:87│ │score:0│ │
                            │  └───┬────┘ └───┬────┘ └──┬───┘ │
                            │      └──────────┴─────────┘     │
                            │              │                   │
                            │     NumberRouter (Sticky)        │
                            └──────────────┬───────────────────┘
                                           │
                                           ▼
                                    ┌─────────────┐
                                    │    REDIS    │
                                    │  (Cola +    │
                                    │  Mapeos)    │
                                    └─────────────┘
```

#### 2.2. Algoritmo de Asignación de Número (NumberRouter)

```
CUANDO llega mensaje del cliente X:
  1. ¿Existe mapeo sticky (X → número)?
     SÍ → ¿Ese número está activo?
            SÍ → Usar ese número
            NO → Romper mapeo, ir a paso 2
     NO → Ir a paso 2
  
  2. Obtener lista de números ACTIVOS ordenados por:
     - health_score (descendente)
     - current_sessions (ascendente) [menor carga]
     - warmup_level (descendente) [preferir números "calentados"]
  
  3. Seleccionar el mejor candidato
  
  4. Crear mapeo sticky: X → número_seleccionado
  
  5. Procesar mensaje por ese número
```

#### 2.3. Health Score (Fórmula)

```python
health_score = (
    base_score *                    # 100 si está activo
    uptime_factor *                 # % de tiempo activo últimas 24h
    (1 / (1 + messages_sent_1h)) *  # Penaliza si envió muchos mensajes recientes
    warmup_factor                   # 0.2 si es nuevo, 1.0 si lleva >7 días
)
```

#### 3.2. GATEWAY: Refactorización Multi-Número

##### 📁 `gateway/src/numberPool.js` (NUEVO - El corazón del sistema)

```javascript
import Redis from 'ioredis';

const redis = new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL || 'redis://redis:6379');

/**
 * NumberPool: Gestiona el pool de números WhatsApp y su estado.
 * 
 * Estados posibles:
 * - pending_qr: Esperando escaneo del QR
 * - connecting: Conectando
 * - active: Operativo
 * - disconnected: Desconectado (reconectando)
 * - burned: Baneado/deslogeado (no usar más)
 */
export class NumberPool {
  constructor() {
    this.connections = new Map(); // alias -> { sock, status, health, stats }
    this.clientMappings = new Map(); // clientPhone -> alias (sticky)
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  // GESTIÓN DE NÚMEROS
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════

  async addNumber(alias) {
    if (this.connections.has(alias)) {
      throw new Error(`El alias '${alias}' ya existe`);
    }

    const entry = {
      alias,
      sock: null,
      status: 'pending_qr',
      qr: null,
      health: {
        score: 0,
        uptime_factor: 0,
        warmup_factor: 0.2,
        messages_sent_1h: 0,
        started_at: Date.now(),
      },
      stats: {
        total_messages: 0,
        active_sessions: 0,
        last_message_at: null,
      },
      created_at: Date.now(),
    };

    this.connections.set(alias, entry);
    
    // Persistir en Redis
    await redis.hset('number_pool:registry', alias, JSON.stringify({
      alias,
      created_at: entry.created_at,
      status: 'pending_qr'
    }));

    console.log(`📱 [NumberPool] Número '${alias}' agregado al pool`);
    return entry;
  }

  async removeNumber(alias) {
    const entry = this.connections.get(alias);
    if (!entry) return false;

    // Cerrar conexión si existe
    if (entry.sock) {
      try {
        await entry.sock.logout();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Error cerrando ${alias}:`, err.message);
      }
    }

    this.connections.delete(alias);
    await redis.hdel('number_pool:registry', alias);
    
    // Liberar mapeos de clientes asignados a este número
    for (const [clientPhone, mappedAlias] of this.clientMappings.entries()) {
      if (mappedAlias === alias) {
        this.clientMappings.delete(clientPhone);
        await redis.hdel('number_pool:client_mappings', clientPhone);
      }
    }

    console.log(`🗑️ [NumberPool] Número '${alias}' removido`);
    return true;
  }

  async markAsBurned(alias, reason = 'logged_out') {
    const entry = this.connections.get(alias);
    if (!entry) return;

    entry.status = 'burned';
    console.log(`🔥 [NumberPool] Número '${alias}' marcado como QUEMADO: ${reason}`);

    await redis.hset('number_pool:registry', alias, JSON.stringify({
      alias,
      status: 'burned',
      burned_at: Date.now(),
      reason
    }));
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  // ROUTING (STICKY + LOAD BALANCING)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════

  async resolveNumberForClient(clientPhone) {
    // 1. Verificar mapeo sticky en memoria
    let alias = this.clientMappings.get(clientPhone);
    
    if (alias) {
      const entry = this.connections.get(alias);
      if (entry && entry.status === 'active') {
        return entry;
      }
      // Mapeo inválido, limpiar
      this.clientMappings.delete(clientPhone);
      await redis.hdel('number_pool:client_mappings', clientPhone);
    }

    // 2. Verificar en Redis (persistencia entre reinicios)
    const storedAlias = await redis.hget('number_pool:client_mappings', clientPhone);
    if (storedAlias) {
      const entry = this.connections.get(storedAlias);
      if (entry && entry.status === 'active') {
        this.clientMappings.set(clientPhone, storedAlias);
        return entry;
      }
      await redis.hdel('number_pool:client_mappings', clientPhone);
    }

    // 3. Seleccionar mejor candidato
    const candidate = this.selectBestCandidate();
    if (!candidate) {
      throw new Error('No hay números disponibles en el pool');
    }

    // 4. Crear mapeo sticky
    this.clientMappings.set(clientPhone, candidate.alias);
    await redis.hset('number_pool:client_mappings', clientPhone, candidate.alias);
    candidate.stats.active_sessions++;

    console.log(`🔗 [Router] Cliente ${clientPhone} → ${candidate.alias}`);
    return candidate;
  }

  selectBestCandidate() {
    const active = Array.from(this.connections.values())
      .filter(e => e.status === 'active');

    if (active.length === 0) return null;

    // Calcular score dinámico y ordenar
    const scored = active.map(entry => {
      const uptimeMs = Date.now() - entry.health.started_at;
      const uptimeHours = uptimeMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
      
      // Warmup factor: crece gradualmente en los primeros 7 días
      const warmup_factor = Math.min(1, uptimeHours / (24 * 7));
      
      // Penalización por carga reciente (última hora)
      const load_penalty = 1 / (1 + entry.health.messages_sent_1h * 0.1);
      
      // Penalización por sesiones activas (balanceo de carga)
      const session_penalty = 1 / (1 + entry.stats.active_sessions * 0.05);
      
      const score = 100 * warmup_factor * load_penalty * session_penalty;
      
      return { entry, score };
    });

    scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    
    // Añadir ruido aleatorio para no ser predecible
    const topCandidates = scored.slice(0, Math.min(3, scored.length));
    const selected = topCandidates[Math.floor(Math.random() * topCandidates.length)];
    
    return selected.entry;
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  // HEALTH MONITORING
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════

  updateHealth(alias, updates) {
    const entry = this.connections.get(alias);
    if (!entry) return;

    Object.assign(entry.health, updates);
    entry.health.score = this.calculateHealthScore(entry);
  }

  calculateHealthScore(entry) {
    const uptimeMs = Date.now() - entry.health.started_at;
    const uptimeHours = uptimeMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    const warmup = Math.min(1, uptimeHours / (24 * 7));
    const loadPenalty = 1 / (1 + entry.health.messages_sent_1h * 0.1);
    
    return Math.round(100 * warmup * loadPenalty);
  }

  recordMessageSent(alias) {
    const entry = this.connections.get(alias);
    if (!entry) return;
    
    entry.health.messages_sent_1h++;
    entry.stats.total_messages++;
    entry.stats.last_message_at = Date.now();
  }

  // Decrementar contador de mensajes por hora cada minuto
  startHealthDecay() {
    setInterval(() => {
      for (const entry of this.connections.values()) {
        if (entry.health.messages_sent_1h > 0) {
          entry.health.messages_sent_1h = Math.max(0, entry.health.messages_sent_1h - 1);
        }
        entry.health.score = this.calculateHealthScore(entry);
      }
    }, 60000); // Cada minuto
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  // ESTADO Y CONSULTAS
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════

  getAll() {
    return Array.from(this.connections.values()).map(entry => ({
      alias: entry.alias,
      status: entry.status,
      health_score: entry.health.score,
      warmup_factor: entry.health.warmup_factor,
      messages_sent_1h: entry.health.messages_sent_1h,
      active_sessions: entry.stats.active_sessions,
      total_messages: entry.stats.total_messages,
      uptime_hours: Math.round((Date.now() - entry.health.started_at) / (1000 * 60 * 60) * 10) / 10,
      created_at: entry.created_at,
    }));
  }

  get(alias) {
    return this.connections.get(alias);
  }

  getByPhone(phone) {
    const alias = this.clientMappings.get(phone);
    if (!alias) return null;
    return this.connections.get(alias);
  }
}

// Instancia singleton
export const numberPool = new NumberPool();
numberPool.startHealthDecay();
```

##### 📁 `gateway/src/whatsapp.js` (REESCRITO - Multi-Connection)

```javascript
import makeWASocket, { 
  useMultiFileAuthState, 
  DisconnectReason,
  fetchLatestBaileysVersion,
  downloadMediaMessage
} from '@whiskeysockets/baileys';
import { Boom } from '@hapi/boom';
import pino from 'pino';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import { numberPool } from './numberPool.js';

const __dirname = path.dirname(path.dirname(new URL(import.meta.url).pathname));
const AUTH_BASE = path.join(__dirname, 'auth_info');

if (!fs.existsSync(AUTH_BASE)) fs.mkdirSync(AUTH_BASE, { recursive: true });

const logger = pino({ level: 'silent' });

/**
 * Inicia una conexión WhatsApp para un alias específico.
 */
export async function initWhatsAppConnection(alias, redis) {
  // Crear o recuperar entrada en el pool
  let entry = numberPool.get(alias);
  if (!entry) {
    entry = await numberPool.addNumber(alias);
  }

  const authDir = path.join(AUTH_BASE, alias);
  if (!fs.existsSync(authDir)) fs.mkdirSync(authDir, { recursive: true });

  const { state, saveCreds } = await useMultiFileAuthState(authDir);
  const { version } = await fetchLatestBaileysVersion();

  const sock = makeWASocket.default({
    version,
    auth: state,
    logger,
    printQRInTerminal: false,
    generateHighQualityLinkPreview: false,
    syncFullHistory: false,
    markOnlineOnConnect: true,
    defaultQueryTimeoutMs: 60000,
    // Anti-detección: comportamiento humano
    browser: ['Chrome', 'Windows', '10.0'],
  });

  entry.sock = sock;
  entry.status = 'connecting';

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  // EVENTOS DE CONEXIÓN
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  sock.ev.on('connection.update', async (update) => {
    const { connection, lastDisconnect, qr } = update;

    if (qr) {
      entry.qr = qr;
      entry.status = 'pending_qr';
      console.log(`\n📱 [${alias}] Escanea este QR:`);
      // Publicar QR en Redis para que el admin lo vea
      await redis.publish('channel:qr_events', JSON.stringify({
        type: 'qr_generated',
        alias,
        qr,
        timestamp: Date.now()
      }));
    }

    if (connection === 'open') {
      entry.status = 'active';
      entry.health.started_at = Date.now();
      entry.qr = null;
      console.log(`✅ [${alias}] WhatsApp conectado y activo`);
      
      await redis.publish('channel:number_events', JSON.stringify({
        type: 'connected',
        alias,
        timestamp: Date.now()
      }));
    }

    if (connection === 'close') {
      const statusCode = new Boom(lastDisconnect?.error)?.output?.statusCode;
      console.error(`❌ [${alias}] Conexión cerrada. Código: ${statusCode}`);

      if (statusCode === DisconnectReason.loggedOut) {
        // BAN DETECTADO: Marcar como quemado
        await numberPool.markAsBurned(alias, 'logged_out');
        entry.status = 'burned';
        
        await redis.publish('channel:number_events', JSON.stringify({
          type: 'burned',
          alias,
          reason: 'logged_out',
          timestamp: Date.now()
        }));
      } else if (entry.status !== 'burned') {
        // Reconexión automática
        entry.status = 'disconnected';
        console.log(`🔄 [${alias}] Reconectando en 5 segundos...`);
        setTimeout(() => initWhatsAppConnection(alias, redis), 5000);
      }
    }
  });

  sock.ev.on('creds.update', saveCreds);

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  // MENSAJES ENTRANTES
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════
  sock.ev.on('messages.upsert', async ({ messages, type }) => {
    if (type !== 'notify') return;

    for (const msg of messages) {
      if (msg.key.fromMe || !msg.message) continue;
      if (msg.key.remoteJid.endsWith('@g.us')) continue;

      const clientPhone = msg.key.remoteJid.split('@')[0];
      let messageContent = '';
      let messageType = 'text';
      let mediaPath = null;

      if (msg.message.conversation) {
        messageContent = msg.message.conversation;
      } else if (msg.message.extendedTextMessage?.text) {
        messageContent = msg.message.extendedTextMessage.text;
      } else if (msg.message.imageMessage) {
        messageType = 'image';
        messageContent = msg.message.imageMessage.caption || '[Imagen]';
        
        try {
          const buffer = await downloadMediaMessage(msg, 'buffer', {});
          const fileName = `${alias}_${clientPhone}_${Date.now()}.jpg`;
          mediaPath = path.join(process.env.SHARED_MEDIA_PATH || '/shared_media', fileName);
          fs.writeFileSync(mediaPath, buffer);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error(`❌ [${alias}] Error descargando imagen:`, err.message);
          messageContent = '[Error al procesar imagen]';
          messageType = 'text';
        }
      } else {
        messageContent = '[Tipo no soportado]';
        messageType = 'other';
      }

      const payload = {
        message_id: uuidv4(),
        timestamp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        from_number: alias,        // ← NUEVO: Qué número recibió el mensaje
        phone: clientPhone,
        message_type: messageType,
        content: messageContent,
        media_path: mediaPath,
        chat_state: 'new'
      };

      await redis.lpush('queue:incoming_messages', JSON.stringify(payload));
      console.log(`📥 [${alias}] Mensaje de ${clientPhone}: "${messageContent.substring(0, 40)}"`);
    }
  });

  return entry;
}

/**
 * Inicializa todos los números registrados al arrancar el gateway.
 */
export async function initAllNumbers(redis) {
  const registry = await redis.hgetall('number_pool:registry');
  const aliases = Object.keys(registry);

  if (aliases.length === 0) {
    console.log('ℹ️ [Gateway] No hay números registrados. Usa el panel admin para agregar uno.');
    return;
  }

  console.log(`🚀 [Gateway] Iniciando ${aliases.length} número(s): ${aliases.join(', ')}`);
  
  for (const alias of aliases) {
    try {
      await initWhatsAppConnection(alias, redis);
      // Esperar un poco entre conexiones para no saturar
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`❌ [Gateway] Error iniciando ${alias}:`, err.message);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Expone el socket de un alias específico para enviar mensajes.
 */
export function getSocket(alias) {
  const entry = numberPool.get(alias);
  return entry?.sock || null;
}
```

##### 📁 `gateway/src/outgoingConsumer.js` (ACTUALIZADO - Respeta from_number)

```javascript
export async function startConsumer(redis) {
  console.log('📤 [Gateway] Consumidor multi-número iniciado.');

  while (true) {
    try {
      const result = await redis.brpop('queue:outgoing_messages', 0);
      if (!result) continue;

      const [, messageStr] = result;
      const message = JSON.parse(messageStr);

      await processOutgoing(message, redis);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('❌ [Gateway] Error en consumidor:', err);
      await sleep(2000);
    }
  }
}

async function processOutgoing(message, redis) {
  const { numberPool } = await import('./numberPool.js');
  const { getSocket } = await import('./whatsapp.js');

  let alias = message.from_number;
  let sock = alias ? getSocket(alias) : null;

  // Si no se especificó número o no está activo, resolver por cliente
  if (!sock) {
    try {
      const entry = await numberPool.resolveNumberForClient(message.phone);
      alias = entry.alias;
      sock = entry.sock;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`❌ [Gateway] No hay números disponibles para ${message.phone}:`, err.message);
      // Notificar al admin
      await redis.publish('channel:admin_alerts', JSON.stringify({
        type: 'no_numbers_available',
        phone: message.phone,
        timestamp: Date.now()
      }));
      return;
    }
  }

  const jid = `${message.phone}@s.whatsapp.net`;

  try {
    switch (message.action) {
      case 'typing_start':
        await sock.sendPresenceUpdate('composing', jid);
        break;

      case 'typing_stop':
        await sock.sendPresenceUpdate('paused', jid);
        break;

      case 'send_message':
        if (message.delay_ms > 0) {
          await sock.sendPresenceUpdate('composing', jid);
          await sleep(message.delay_ms);
          await sock.sendPresenceUpdate('paused', jid);
        }
        
        await sock.sendMessage(jid, { text: message.content });
        
        // Registrar envío en health stats
        numberPool.recordMessageSent(alias);
        
        console.log(`📤 [${alias}] → ${message.phone}: "${message.content.substring(0, 40)}"`);
        break;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`❌ [${alias}] Error enviando a ${message.phone}:`, err.message);
    
    // Si el error es de conexión, intentar con otro número
    if (err.message?.includes('connection') || err.message?.includes('closed')) {
      console.log(`🔄 [${alias}] Fallo de conexión, reintentando con otro número...`);
      message.from_number = null; // Forzar re-resolución
      await redis.lpush('queue:outgoing_messages', JSON.stringify(message));
    }
  }
}

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
```

##### 📁 `gateway/src/index.js` (ACTUALIZADO)

```javascript
import { initAllNumbers, initWhatsAppConnection } from './whatsapp.js';
import { startConsumer } from './outgoingConsumer.js';
import { numberPool } from './numberPool.js';
import { startAdminAPI } from './adminApi.js';
import Redis from 'ioredis';

const redis = new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL || 'redis://redis:6379');

console.log('🚀 [Gateway] Iniciando Multi-Number WhatsApp Gateway...');

// 1. Iniciar API interna para que el panel admin gestione números
startAdminAPI(redis);

// 2. Iniciar todas las conexiones WhatsApp registradas
initAllNumbers(redis);

// 3. Iniciar consumidor de cola de salida
startConsumer(redis);

// 4. Escuchar comandos de admin (agregar/quitar números)
(async () => {
  const sub = redis.duplicate();
  await sub.subscribe('channel:admin_commands');
  
  sub.on('message', async (channel, messageStr) => {
    try {
      const cmd = JSON.parse(messageStr);
      
      switch (cmd.action) {
        case 'add_number':
          await initWhatsAppConnection(cmd.alias, redis);
          break;
        case 'remove_number':
          await numberPool.removeNumber(cmd.alias);
          break;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error procesando comando admin:', err);
    }
  });
})();
```

##### 📁 `gateway/src/adminApi.js` (NUEVO - API interna para el panel)

```javascript
import http from 'http';
import { numberPool } from './numberPool.js';

/**
 * Mini API HTTP interna para que el panel admin gestione el pool de números.
 * Corre en el puerto 3001 dentro del contenedor del gateway.
 */
export function startAdminAPI(redis) {
  const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
    res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');
    res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, DELETE, OPTIONS');
    res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type');
    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');

    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
      res.writeHead(200);
      res.end();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const url = new URL(req.url, `http://${req.headers.host}`);
      const path = url.pathname;

      // GET /numbers - Listar todos los números
      if (path === '/numbers' && req.method === 'GET') {
        const numbers = numberPool.getAll();
        res.writeHead(200);
        res.end(JSON.stringify(numbers));
        return;
      }

      // POST /numbers - Agregar nuevo número
      if (path === '/numbers' && req.method === 'POST') {
        const body = await readBody(req);
        const { alias } = JSON.parse(body);
        
        if (!alias || !/^[a-z0-9_-]+$/i.test(alias)) {
          res.writeHead(400);
          res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Alias inválido. Usa solo letras, números, _ o -' }));
          return;
        }

        await numberPool.addNumber(alias);
        
        // Publicar comando para que el index.js inicie la conexión
        await redis.publish('channel:admin_commands', JSON.stringify({
          action: 'add_number',
          alias
        }));

        res.writeHead(201);
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ status: 'created', alias }));
        return;
      }

      // DELETE /numbers/:alias - Remover número
      if (path.startsWith('/numbers/') && req.method === 'DELETE') {
        const alias = path.split('/')[2];
        const removed = await numberPool.removeNumber(alias);
        
        if (removed) {
          await redis.publish('channel:admin_commands', JSON.stringify({
            action: 'remove_number',
            alias
          }));
        }

        res.writeHead(removed ? 200 : 404);
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ status: removed ? 'removed' : 'not_found' }));
        return;
      }

      // GET /numbers/:alias/qr - Obtener QR pendiente
      if (path.match(/^\/numbers\/[^/]+\/qr$/) && req.method === 'GET') {
        const alias = path.split('/')[2];
        const entry = numberPool.get(alias);
        
        if (!entry) {
          res.writeHead(404);
          res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Número no encontrado' }));
          return;
        }

        res.writeHead(200);
        res.end(JSON.stringify({ alias, qr: entry.qr, status: entry.status }));
        return;
      }

      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Not found' }));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[AdminAPI] Error:', err);
      res.writeHead(500);
      res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message }));
    }
  });

  server.listen(3001, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log('🔌 [AdminAPI] Escuchando en puerto 3001');
  });
}

function readBody(req) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let body = '';
    req.on('data', chunk => body += chunk);
    req.on('end', () => resolve(body));
    req.on('error', reject);
  });
}
```

```python
async def process_message(message: dict):
    phone = message["phone"]
    content = message["content"]
    msg_type = message.get("message_type", "text")
    media_path = message.get("media_path")
    from_number = message.get("from_number")  # ← NUEVO: Qué número recibió
    
    # ... (todo el procesamiento existente igual)
    
    # Al enviar respuesta al gateway, incluir from_number
    await send_to_gateway(
        phone=phone,
        content=ai_response,
        action="send_message",
        delay_ms=delay_ms,
        msg_id=message["message_id"],
        from_number=from_number  # ← NUEVO
    )


async def send_to_gateway(phone: str, content: str, action: str, 
                          delay_ms: int, msg_id: str, from_number: str = None):
    """Helper para enviar mensajes a la cola de salida."""
    payload = {
        "message_id": msg_id,
        "phone": phone,
        "content": content,
        "action": action,
        "delay_ms": delay_ms
    }
    if from_number:
        payload["from_number"] = from_number
    
    await redis_client.lpush("queue:outgoing_messages", json.dumps(payload))
```

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/router.py` (AGREGAR endpoints de números)

Agrega estos nuevos endpoints al router admin existente:

```python
import httpx
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

# Al inicio del archivo, después de los imports existentes:
GATEWAY_ADMIN_URL = "http://bot_gateway:3001"

# ═══════════════════════════════════════
# GESTIÓN DE NÚMEROS WHATSAPP
# ═══════════════════════════════════════

@router.get("/numbers")
async def list_numbers(admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    """Lista todos los números WhatsApp del pool con su estado."""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=5.0) as client:
        resp = await client.get(f"{GATEWAY_ADMIN_URL}/numbers")
        return resp.json()

@router.post("/numbers")
async def add_number(alias: str, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    """Agrega un nuevo número al pool. Devuelve el QR para escanear."""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10.0) as client:
        resp = await client.post(
            f"{GATEWAY_ADMIN_URL}/numbers",
            json={"alias": alias}
        )
        if resp.status_code != 201:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=resp.status_code, detail=resp.json())
        return resp.json()

@router.delete("/numbers/{alias}")
async def remove_number(alias: str, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    """Remueve un número del pool."""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=5.0) as client:
        resp = await client.delete(f"{GATEWAY_ADMIN_URL}/numbers/{alias}")
        return resp.json()

@router.get("/numbers/{alias}/qr")
async def get_number_qr(alias: str, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    """Obtiene el QR pendiente de un número."""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=5.0) as client:
        resp = await client.get(f"{GATEWAY_ADMIN_URL}/numbers/{alias}/qr")
        return resp.json()
```

#### 3.4. FRONTEND: Página de Gestión de Números

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/pages/Numbers.jsx` (NUEVO)

```jsx
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import api from '../api/client'
import { chatWs } from '../api/websocket'
import { 
  Smartphone, Plus, Trash2, QrCode, CheckCircle2, 
  XCircle, AlertTriangle, Wifi, WifiOff
} from 'lucide-react'

export default function Numbers() {
  const [numbers, setNumbers] = useState([])
  const [newAlias, setNewAlias] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [qrModal, setQrModal] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    loadNumbers()
    
    // Escuchar eventos en tiempo real
    const unsub = chatWs.subscribe((data) => {
      if (data.type === 'qr_generated' || data.type === 'connected' || data.type === 'burned') {
        loadNumbers()
        if (data.type === 'qr_generated') {
          setQrModal({ alias: data.alias, qr: data.qr })
        }
      }
    })
    
    return unsub
  }, [])

  const loadNumbers = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/api/admin/numbers')
      setNumbers(res.data)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }

  const handleAdd = async () => {
    if (!newAlias.trim()) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/admin/numbers?alias=${encodeURIComponent(newAlias)}`)
      setNewAlias('')
      loadNumbers()
    } catch (err) {
      alert(err.response?.data?.detail || 'Error al agregar número')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const handleRemove = async (alias) => {
    if (!confirm(`¿Eliminar el número '${alias}'? Esta acción no se puede deshacer.`)) return
    try {
      await api.delete(`/api/admin/numbers/${alias}`)
      loadNumbers()
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Error al eliminar')
    }
  }

  const handleShowQR = async (alias) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/api/admin/numbers/${alias}/qr`)
      if (res.data.qr) {
        setQrModal({ alias, qr: res.data.qr })
      } else {
        alert('Este número no tiene un QR pendiente')
      }
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Error obteniendo QR')
    }
  }

  const getStatusInfo = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'active': return { icon: CheckCircle2, color: 'text-green-500', label: 'Activo' }
      case 'pending_qr': return { icon: QrCode, color: 'text-yellow-500', label: 'Esperando QR' }
      case 'connecting': return { icon: Wifi, color: 'text-blue-500', label: 'Conectando' }
      case 'disconnected': return { icon: WifiOff, color: 'text-orange-500', label: 'Desconectado' }
      case 'burned': return { icon: XCircle, color: 'text-red-500', label: 'Baneado' }
      default: return { icon: AlertTriangle, color: 'text-gray-500', label: status }
    }
  }

  const getHealthColor = (score) => {
    if (score >= 80) return 'bg-green-500'
    if (score >= 50) return 'bg-yellow-500'
    if (score > 0) return 'bg-orange-500'
    return 'bg-red-500'
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Gestión de Números</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mt-1">
            Administra el pool de números WhatsApp. Sistema con rotación automática y sticky sessions.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Agregar nuevo número */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-lg shadow mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Agregar nuevo número</h2>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={newAlias}
            onChange={(e) => setNewAlias(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ej: numero1, ventas_principal, soporte_2"
            className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
          />
          <button
            onClick={handleAdd}
            disabled={loading || !newAlias.trim()}
            className="px-6 py-2 bg-primary-500 hover:bg-primary-600 text-white rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Plus className="w-5 h-5" />
            Agregar
          </button>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">
          💡 El alias es un identificador interno (no el número de teléfono). Úsalo para recordar qué SIM es.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Lista de números */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {numbers.map((num) => {
          const statusInfo = getStatusInfo(num.status)
          const StatusIcon = statusInfo.icon
          
          return (
            <div key={num.alias} className="bg-white p-5 rounded-lg shadow border-l-4 border-primary-500">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Smartphone className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-600" />
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{num.alias}</h3>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleRemove(num.alias)}
                  className="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700"
                  title="Eliminar número"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Estado */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <StatusIcon className={`w-5 h-5 ${statusInfo.color}`} />
                <span className={`text-sm font-medium ${statusInfo.color}`}>
                  {statusInfo.label}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Health Score */}
              <div className="mb-3">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-600 mb-1">
                  <span>Health Score</span>
                  <span className="font-bold">{num.health_score}/100</span>
                </div>
                <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2">
                  <div
                    className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${getHealthColor(num.health_score)}`}
                    style={{ width: `${num.health_score}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Stats */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs mb-3">
                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded">
                  <div className="text-gray-500">Sesiones activas</div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-lg">{num.active_sessions}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded">
                  <div className="text-gray-500">Mensajes/h</div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-lg">{num.messages_sent_1h}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded">
                  <div className="text-gray-500">Uptime</div>
                  <div className="font-bold">{num.uptime_hours}h</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded">
                  <div className="text-gray-500">Total msgs</div>
                  <div className="font-bold">{num.total_messages}</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Acciones */}
              {num.status === 'pending_qr' && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleShowQR(num.alias)}
                  className="w-full py-2 bg-yellow-500 hover:bg-yellow-600 text-white rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  <QrCode className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Ver QR
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )
        })}

        {numbers.length === 0 && (
          <div className="col-span-full text-center py-12 text-gray-400">
            <Smartphone className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-50" />
            <p>No hay números registrados</p>
            <p className="text-sm">Agrega tu primer número WhatsApp arriba</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Modal QR */}
      {qrModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-lg max-w-md w-full">
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Escanea este QR</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-4">
              Número: <strong>{qrModal.alias}</strong>
            </p>
            <div className="bg-gray-100 p-4 rounded-lg mb-4 font-mono text-xs break-all max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
              {qrModal.qr}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-4">
              💡 Abre WhatsApp → Dispositivos vinculados → Vincular dispositivo
            </p>
            <button
              onClick={() => setQrModal(null)}
              className="w-full py-2 bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 rounded-lg"
            >
              Cerrar
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

### 🧪 4. Protocolo de Pruebas

#### Paso 1: Reiniciar todos los servicios
```bash
docker-compose down
docker-compose up -d
```

#### Paso 2: Agregar el primer número
1. Abre el panel: `http://localhost:5173`
2. Ve a **Números WhatsApp**
3. Agrega un alias: `numero1`
4. Haz clic en "Ver QR" y escanéalo con tu WhatsApp

#### Paso 3: Agregar un segundo número (para probar rotación)
1. Agrega otro alias: `numero2`
2. Escanea el QR con **otro WhatsApp** (necesitas otra SIM)
3. Verás ambos números en el panel con health score creciendo

#### Paso 4: Probar sticky session
1. Desde un tercer teléfono, escribe al número 1
2. El bot responde desde el número 1 (verás el mapeo en consola)
3. Escribe de nuevo → sigue respondiendo el número 1 (sticky)

#### Paso 5: Probar rotación automática
1. Escribe desde un teléfono **nuevo** (sin mapeo previo)
2. El sistema asignará el número con mejor health score
3. Verás en la consola: `🔗 [Router] Cliente XXX → numero2`

#### Paso 6: Probar detección de ban (simulado)
1. En tu WhatsApp físico, ve a "Dispositivos vinculados" y cierra sesión del número 1
2. En el panel verás que el número cambia a estado "Baneado"
3. El tráfico se redirige automáticamente al número 2
4. El número quemado queda marcado y no se usa más

---

### 📊 5. Métricas de Resiliencia (CTO)

| Escenario | Comportamiento del Sistema | Tiempo de recuperación |
|-----------|---------------------------|------------------------|
| 1 número cae | Tráfico se redirige a otros | < 5 segundos |
| Todos los números caen | Se encolan mensajes + alerta admin | Manual (agregar número) |
| Cliente cambia de número | Sticky session mantiene contexto | 0 (transparente) |
| Número nuevo | Warmup progresivo (7 días) | Automático |
| Cliente escribe de noche | Delay más largo para naturalidad | Automático |
