#### 3.9. Endpoints Admin para Gestión de Tickets

##### 📁 `orchestrator/app/admin/router.py` (AGREGAR)

```python
from app.tickets.core import (
    get_open_ticket_for_client, resolve_ticket, close_ticket,
    escalate_ticket, add_ticket_message, get_ticket_messages
)
from app.tickets.knowledge import create_kb_article
from app.db import SupportTicket, TicketMessage, KnowledgeBaseArticle

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════
# GESTIÓN DE TICKETS
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════

@router.get("/tickets")
async def list_tickets(
    status: str = None,
    priority: str = None,
    level: str = None,
    limit: int = 50,
    admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    """Lista tickets con filtros."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        query = select(SupportTicket)
        
        if status:
            query = query.where(SupportTicket.status == status)
        if priority:
            query = query.where(SupportTicket.priority == priority)
        if level:
            query = query.where(SupportTicket.level == level)
        
        query = query.order_by(SupportTicket.created_at.desc()).limit(limit)
        
        result = await session.execute(query)
        tickets = result.scalars().all()
        
        return [
            {
                "id": t.id,
                "ticket_code": t.ticket_code,
                "client_phone": t.client_phone,
                "client_name": t.client_name,
                "category": t.category,
                "priority": t.priority,
                "status": t.status,
                "level": t.level,
                "platform": t.platform,
                "subject": t.subject,
                "created_at": t.created_at.isoformat(),
                "sla_response": t.sla_response_deadline.isoformat(),
                "sla_resolution": t.sla_resolution_deadline.isoformat(),
                "satisfaction": t.satisfaction_score
            }
            for t in tickets
        ]

@router.get("/tickets/{ticket_id}")
async def get_ticket_detail(ticket_id: int, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    """Detalle completo de un ticket."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            select(SupportTicket).where(SupportTicket.id == ticket_id)
        )
        ticket = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        
        if not ticket:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Ticket no encontrado")
        
        messages = await get_ticket_messages(ticket_id)
        
        return {
            "ticket": {
                "id": ticket.id,
                "ticket_code": ticket.ticket_code,
                "client_phone": ticket.client_phone,
                "client_name": ticket.client_name,
                "category": ticket.category,
                "priority": ticket.priority,
                "status": ticket.status,
                "level": ticket.level,
                "platform": ticket.platform,
                "subject": ticket.subject,
                "description": ticket.description,
                "resolution_type": ticket.resolution_type,
                "resolution_notes": ticket.resolution_notes,
                "satisfaction": ticket.satisfaction_score,
                "created_at": ticket.created_at.isoformat(),
                "resolved_at": ticket.resolved_at.isoformat() if ticket.resolved_at else None
            },
            "messages": messages
        }

@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/resolve")
async def resolve_ticket_endpoint(
    ticket_id: int,
    resolution_notes: str,
    resolution_type: str = "human",
    admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    """Resuelve un ticket manualmente."""
    result = await resolve_ticket(ticket_id, resolution_type, resolution_notes, str(admin.id))
    
    if "error" in result:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=result["error"])
    
    # Enviar mensaje de resolución al cliente
    async with async_session() as session:
        t_result = await session.execute(
            select(SupportTicket).where(SupportTicket.id == ticket_id)
        )
        ticket = t_result.scalar_one_or_none()
    
    if ticket:
        import redis.asyncio as redis
        from app.config import settings
        redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
        
        msg = (
            f"✅ Tu ticket {ticket.ticket_code} ha sido resuelto.\n\n"
            f"📝 {resolution_notes}\n\n"
            f"¿Cómo calificarías tu experiencia? Responde del 1 al 5 ⭐"
        )
        
        await redis_client.lpush("queue:outgoing_messages", json.dumps({
            "message_id": f"ticket_resolved_{ticket_id}",
            "phone": ticket.client_phone,
            "content": msg,
            "action": "send_message",
            "delay_ms": 0
        }))
    
    # Auto-crear KB article
    from app.tickets.knowledge import auto_create_kb_from_ticket
    await auto_create_kb_from_ticket(ticket_id, resolution_notes)
    
    return result

@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/message")
async def add_message_to_ticket(
    ticket_id: int,
    content: str,
    admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)
):
    """Envía un mensaje al cliente desde el ticket."""
    result = await add_ticket_message(
        ticket_id, "agent", content, sender_id=str(admin.id)
    )
    
    # Enviar por WhatsApp
    async with async_session() as session:
        t_result = await session.execute(
            select(SupportTicket).where(SupportTicket.id == ticket_id)
        )
        ticket = t_result.scalar_one_or_none()
    
    if ticket:
        import redis.asyncio as redis
        from app.config import settings
        redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
        
        await redis_client.lpush("queue:outgoing_messages", json.dumps({
            "message_id": f"ticket_msg_{ticket_id}",
            "phone": ticket.client_phone,
            "content": content,
            "action": "send_message",
            "delay_ms": 0
        }))
    
    return result

@router.get("/tickets/metrics")
async def ticket_metrics(days: int = 30, admin: Admin = Depends(get_current_admin)):
    """Métricas de soporte."""
    from datetime import timedelta
    start_date = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(days=days)
    
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Total tickets
        total = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(SupportTicket.id)).where(
                SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date
            )
        )
        total = total.scalar() or 0
        
        # Resueltos por bot
        bot_resolved = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(SupportTicket.id)).where(
                and_(
                    SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date,
                    SupportTicket.resolution_type == "bot_auto"
                )
            )
        )
        bot_resolved = bot_resolved.scalar() or 0
        
        # Resueltos por humano
        human_resolved = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(SupportTicket.id)).where(
                and_(
                    SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date,
                    SupportTicket.resolution_type.in_(["human", "replacement"])
                )
            )
        )
        human_resolved = human_resolved.scalar() or 0
        
        # Tiempo promedio de resolución
        avg_time = await session.execute(
            select(func.avg(
                func.extract('epoch', SupportTicket.resolved_at - SupportTicket.created_at) / 60
            )).where(
                and_(
                    SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date,
                    SupportTicket.resolved_at != None
                )
            )
        )
        avg_time = avg_time.scalar() or 0
        
        # Satisfacción promedio
        avg_satisfaction = await session.execute(
            select(func.avg(SupportTicket.satisfaction_score)).where(
                and_(
                    SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date,
                    SupportTicket.satisfaction_score != None
                )
            )
        )
        avg_satisfaction = avg_satisfaction.scalar() or 0
        
        # Tickets por categoría
        by_category = await session.execute(
            select(SupportTicket.category, func.count(SupportTicket.id))
            .where(SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date)
            .group_by(SupportTicket.category)
        )
        
        # Tickets abiertos
        open_count = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(SupportTicket.id)).where(
                SupportTicket.status.in_(["open", "in_progress", "escalated", "waiting_client"])
            )
        )
        open_count = open_count.scalar() or 0
        
        # SLA cumplido
        sla_met = await session.execute(
            select(func.count(SupportTicket.id)).where(
                and_(
                    SupportTicket.created_at >= start_date,
                    SupportTicket.first_response_at != None,
                    SupportTicket.first_response_at <= SupportTicket.sla_response_deadline
                )
            )
        )
        sla_met = sla_met.scalar() or 0
        
        return {
            "period_days": days,
            "total_tickets": total,
            "open_tickets": open_count,
            "bot_resolved": bot_resolved,
            "human_resolved": human_resolved,
            "bot_resolution_rate": round(bot_resolved / max(1, bot_resolved + human_resolved) * 100, 1),
            "avg_resolution_time_min": round(avg_time, 1),
            "avg_satisfaction": round(avg_satisfaction, 2),
            "sla_compliance": round(sla_met / max(1, total) * 100, 1),
            "by_category": {cat: count for cat, count in by_category}
        }
```

#### 3.10. Frontend: Panel de Tickets

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/pages/Tickets.jsx` (NUEVO)

```jsx
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import api from '../api/client'
import { 
  Ticket, Clock, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, 
  MessageSquare, Star, ArrowUp, Send, Filter
} from 'lucide-react'

const PRIORITY_COLORS = {
  critical: 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300',
  high: 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300',
  medium: 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border-yellow-300',
  low: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-300'
}

const STATUS_COLORS = {
  open: 'bg-blue-500',
  in_progress: 'bg-purple-500',
  waiting_client: 'bg-yellow-500',
  escalated: 'bg-orange-500',
  resolved: 'bg-green-500',
  closed: 'bg-gray-500'
}

export default function Tickets() {
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([])
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState(null)
  const [selectedTicket, setSelectedTicket] = useState(null)
  const [ticketDetail, setTicketDetail] = useState(null)
  const [replyText, setReplyText] = useState('')
  const [resolveText, setResolveText] = useState('')
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('open')

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTickets()
    loadMetrics()
    const interval = setInterval(loadTickets, 15000)
    return () => clearInterval(interval)
  }, [filter])

  const loadTickets = async () => {
    try {
      const status = filter === 'all' ? undefined : filter
      const res = await api.get('/api/admin/tickets', { params: { status } })
      setTickets(res.data)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }

  const loadMetrics = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/api/admin/tickets/metrics')
      setMetrics(res.data)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }

  const loadTicketDetail = async (ticketId) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/api/admin/tickets/${ticketId}`)
      setTicketDetail(res.data)
      setSelectedTicket(ticketId)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
    }
  }

  const handleReply = async () => {
    if (!replyText.trim() || !selectedTicket) return
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/admin/tickets/${selectedTicket}/message`, null, {
        params: { content: replyText }
      })
      setReplyText('')
      loadTicketDetail(selectedTicket)
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Error: ' + err.message)
    }
  }

  const handleResolve = async () => {
    if (!resolveText.trim() || !selectedTicket) return
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/admin/tickets/${selectedTicket}/resolve`, null, {
        params: { resolution_notes: resolveText, resolution_type: 'human' }
      })
      setResolveText('')
      loadTicketDetail(selectedTicket)
      loadTickets()
      loadMetrics()
      alert('✅ Ticket resuelto y cliente notificado')
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Error: ' + err.message)
    }
  }

  if (!metrics) return <div className="p-6">Cargando...</div>

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 pb-24 md:pb-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-4">Tickets de Soporte</h1>

      {/* Métricas */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow text-center">
          <Ticket className="w-5 h-5 mx-auto text-blue-500 mb-1" />
          <p className="text-lg font-bold">{metrics.total_tickets}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Total</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow text-center">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 mx-auto text-orange-500 mb-1" />
          <p className="text-lg font-bold">{metrics.open_tickets}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Abiertos</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow text-center">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 mx-auto text-green-500 mb-1" />
          <p className="text-lg font-bold">{metrics.bot_resolution_rate}%</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Bot resolvió</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow text-center">
          <Clock className="w-5 h-5 mx-auto text-purple-500 mb-1" />
          <p className="text-lg font-bold">{metrics.avg_resolution_time_min}m</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Tiempo promedio</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow text-center">
          <Star className="w-5 h-5 mx-auto text-yellow-500 mb-1" />
          <p className="text-lg font-bold">{metrics.avg_satisfaction}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Satisfacción</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow text-center">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 mx-auto text-blue-500 mb-1" />
          <p className="text-lg font-bold">{metrics.sla_compliance}%</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">SLA cumplido</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filtros */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
        {['open', 'escalated', 'resolved', 'closed', 'all'].map(s => (
          <button
            key={s}
            onClick={() => setFilter(s)}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm whitespace-nowrap capitalize ${
              filter === s ? 'bg-primary-500 text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'
            }`}
          >
            {s === 'all' ? 'Todos' : s.replace('_', ' ')}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Layout: Lista + Detalle */}
      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-4">
        {/* Lista de tickets */}
        <div className="lg:w-1/2 bg-white rounded-lg shadow overflow-hidden">
          <div className="divide-y max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto">
            {tickets.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-center text-gray-500 py-8">No hay tickets</p>
            ) : (
              tickets.map(t => (
                <div
                  key={t.id}
                  onClick={() => loadTicketDetail(t.id)}
                  className={`p-3 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50 transition ${
                    selectedTicket === t.id ? 'bg-blue-50 border-l-4 border-primary-500' : ''
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${STATUS_COLORS[t.status]}`} />
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold">{t.ticket_code}</span>
                      <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-xs border ${PRIORITY_COLORS[t.priority]}`}>
                        {t.priority}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                      {new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleString('es-CO', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{t.subject}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                    {t.client_name} • {t.platform || 'N/A'} • {t.category}
                  </p>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Detalle del ticket */}
        <div className="lg:w-1/2 bg-white rounded-lg shadow">
          {ticketDetail ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col h-[600px]">
              {/* Header */}
              <div className="p-4 border-b">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                  <h3 className="font-bold">{ticketDetail.ticket.ticket_code}</h3>
                  <span className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs ${PRIORITY_COLORS[ticketDetail.ticket.priority]}`}>
                    {ticketDetail.ticket.priority}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">{ticketDetail.ticket.subject}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                  {ticketDetail.ticket.client_name} • {ticketDetail.ticket.client_phone} • Nivel: {ticketDetail.ticket.level}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Mensajes */}
              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-3">
                {ticketDetail.messages.map(m => (
                  <div
                    key={m.id}
                    className={`p-3 rounded-lg max-w-[85%] ${
                      m.sender === 'client' 
                        ? 'bg-gray-100 ml-auto' 
                        : m.is_internal 
                          ? 'bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200'
                          : 'bg-blue-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-1 capitalize">
                      {m.sender} • {new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('es-CO')}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm">{m.content}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Acciones */}
              {ticketDetail.ticket.status !== 'closed' && (
                <div className="p-4 border-t space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={replyText}
                      onChange={(e) => setReplyText(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Escribe un mensaje al cliente..."
                      className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm"
                      onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleReply()}
                    />
                    <button onClick={handleReply} className="p-2 bg-primary-500 text-white rounded-lg">
                      <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={resolveText}
                      onChange={(e) => setResolveText(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Notas de resolución..."
                      className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm"
                    />
                    <button
                      onClick={handleResolve}
                      className="px-3 py-2 bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                      Resolver
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-[600px] text-gray-400">
              Selecciona un ticket para ver el detalle
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

---

##### 📁 `admin-panel/src/App.jsx` (ACTUALIZADO)

```jsx
import Tickets from './pages/Tickets'

// En <Routes>:
<Route path="/tickets" element={<ProtectedRoute><Tickets /></ProtectedRoute>} />
```

---

### 🧪 4. Protocolo de Pruebas

#### Paso 1: Probar creación automática de ticket
Desde un teléfono:
1. Envía: "No me funciona Netflix, sale error NW-2-5"
2. El bot debe:
   - Crear ticket `TK-20261005-XXXX`
   - Clasificar: categoría `error_stream`, prioridad `high`
   - Buscar solución en KB
   - Responder con los pasos de solución

#### Paso 2: Probar resolución por bot
1. Responde: "SÍ" (la solución funcionó)
2. El bot cierra el ticket y pide calificación
3. Responde: "5"
4. Verifica en el panel que el ticket está cerrado con ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐

#### Paso 3: Probar escalación a humano
1. Crea otro ticket de soporte
2. Responde: "NO" (la solución no funcionó)
3. El bot debe escalar a Nivel 2
4. Debes recibir push notification en tu celular

#### Paso 4: Probar resolución manual
1. Abre el panel → Tickets
2. Selecciona el ticket escalado
3. Escribe un mensaje al cliente
4. Escribe notas de resolución y haz click en "Resolver"
5. El cliente recibe notificación de resolución

#### Paso 5: Probar SLA
1. Crea un ticket crítico (usa "no funciona nada, perdí todo")
2. No lo resuelvas
3. En 10 minutos, el SLA worker debe escalar automáticamente
4. Verifica la alerta en el panel

#### Paso 6: Ver métricas
1. Abre el panel → Tickets
2. Revisa las métricas: tasa de resolución por bot, tiempo promedio, satisfacción

---

### 📊 5. Métricas de Soporte (CTO)

| Métrica | Antes (Sprint 12) | Después (Sprint 13) | Meta 60 días |
|---------|-------------------|---------------------|-------------|
| Resolución por bot | ~40% | ~70% | 80% |
| Tiempo resolución | 15-30 min | 3-8 min | < 5 min |
| Satisfacción cliente | No medida | 4.2/5 | > 4.5 |
| Cumplimiento SLA | 0% | 85% | > 95% |
| Tickets sin resolver | ~20% | < 5% | < 2% |
| Intervención humana | ~60% | ~30% | < 20% |

---

### 🎯 7. Valor Estratégico (CTO)

1. **Profesionalización total:** Tu soporte ahora rivaliza con empresas de telecomunicaciones.
2. **Base de conocimiento viva:** Cada ticket resuelto hace al bot más inteligente. En 6 meses, el bot resolverá el 90% de los problemas sin intervención.
3. **SLAs = Confianza:** Los clientes saben que su problema será atendido en un tiempo definido. Esto reduce el churn.
4. **Datos accionables:** Sabes exactamente cuáles son los problemas más frecuentes y puedes prevenirlos (ej: si 40% de los tickets son por contraseñas, busca un proveedor con contraseñas más estables).
5. **Escalabilidad del equipo:** Cuando contrates agentes de soporte, ya tienes el sistema listo. Solo los asignas a tickets de Nivel 2/3.
